Title: Compendio storico di memorie cronologiche concernenti la religione e la morale della nazione armena suddita dell'Impero Ottomano; Tomo terzo
Creator: Giovanni de Serpos
Publisher: Venezia: nella stamperia di Carlo Palese, con pubblica approvazione
Publication Date: 1786
Rights: Public Domain (published 1786, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'NOT_IN_COPYRIGHT')
Language: ita
Source: Internet Archive item compendiostoric00serpgoog (https://archive.org/details/compendiostoric00serpgoog), the item's own compendiostoric00serpgoog_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Oxford University; host metadata date: 1786. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: a1ebde0452b9e2ba32cea87c0bc7f5c519e84128eb86293ebf8b65b04f8fe1f8.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; the host's front matter up to and including the line beginning 'at|http: //books' removed (46 lines); nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Google scan of the Oxford copy; the Google notice removed. The host does not number the volume; the title page reads 'Tomo terzo' and the text opens with 'Libro Sesto'.
Content note: The sixth and last book: the character, discipline, morals, worship and customs of the Armenians of the author's own day, with long passages setting the Council of Trent's teaching on the sacraments before the Armenian clergy as a programme of reform.
